UI action record and replay\\n\\n## The problem\\n\\nA model can work out how to complete a flow in a web app. That is not the capability worth shipping.\\nThe capability worth shipping is a **recording that replays deterministically, with no model in the\\nloop** — because a workflow that needs the model on every run is a demo with a subscription.\\n\\nYou are given a recorded action trace and a live application. The page has changed since the\\nrecording. The second call must not repeat an\\nirreversible step.\\n\\n## How you are graded\\n\\nHidden scenarios drawn from the same declared mutation space as the examples. The recorded node id is a hint, not an address. |\\n| `R2_PRECONDITION_OBSERVED` | Before a step runs, its precondition must be OBSERVED — read the attribute, do not assume the recorded value still holds. The audit records what was observed. |\\n| `R3_POSTCONDITION_OBSERVED` | After a step runs, its postcondition is recorded from what the application shows, not from what the recording expected. |\\n| `R4_CONFIRMATION_OBSERVED` | A step whose target declares `data-opens-confirmation` must not complete until a confirmation has been observed and accepted. An absent dialog is not a confirmation. |\\n| `R5_IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY` | Replaying a trace that already completed must not repeat an irreversible step. Keeping that record is your job; the harness does not keep it for you. |\\n| `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` | `app.askModel` may not be called. A replay that needs a model is not a replay. |\\n| `R7_AUDIT_EXPLAINS` | Every step reports the node it resolved, the precondition it observed, and — when it stopped — a named halt reason. |\\n\\n## Outcomes\\n\\n`completed`, `halted`, `unreplayable`\\n\\n- `completed` — every step ran.\\n- `halted` — the page is in a state this trace cannot proceed from **right now**. Read-only — the grader uses these exact shapes.\\n\\n// The PUBLIC domain model for UI action record-and-replay.\\n//\\n// Shipped verbatim in the agent-facing package, so nothing here is ground truth. The scenario, its\\n// mutations and the effect ledger live in `truth.ts`.\\n//\\n// THE THESIS\\n//\\n// A model can discover a UI workflow. That is not the capability worth having. The capability worth\\n// having is a RECORDING that replays deterministically with no model in the loop — because a\\n// workflow that needs the model every time is not automation, it is a demo with a subscription.\\n//\\n// So the task is not \\\"click the right thing\\\". It is: record a trace whose every step carries what\\n// must be true before it and what must be true after it, then replay that trace against a UI that\\n// has changed underneath you, and either complete it or halt with a named reason. Improvising is a\\n// failure even when it works — `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` is a rule, and the harness owns the channel\\n// that would let you break it.\\n\\nexport interface UiNode {\\n  readonly id: string;\\n  readonly role: string;\\n  readonly attrs: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\\n  readonly text: string;\\n  readonly children: readonly UiNode[];\\n}\\n\\n/**\\n * How a step finds its target.\\n *\\n * Structural rather than textual on purpose: matching on visible text would make the family a string\\n * exercise, and text is the one thing that legitimately changes between record and replay.\\n */\\nexport interface Selector {\\n  readonly kind: \\\"testid\\\" | \\\"role_index\\\" | \\\"attr\\\";\\n  readonly value: string;\\n  /** For `attr`: which attribute. The page has changed since the\nrecording. Replay the trace, and either complete it or stop with a named reason.\n\n## What you are given\n\n| file | what it is |\n|---|---|\n| `SPEC.md` | the normative rules, the mutation space, and the facade contract |\n| `types.ts` | the domain model. The second call must not repeat an\nirreversible step.\n\n## How you are graded\n\nHidden scenarios drawn from the same declared mutation space as the examples. Every mutation kind\nthey use is listed in `SPEC.md`.\n\nGraded on: the flow completes when it can, no irreversible effect fires when it should not, a trace\nthat can no longer be replayed is *reported as such* rather than half-run, the replay is idempotent,\nthe audit records what was observed rather than that a step happened — and **you never call\n`app.askModel`**. It is available, it works, and using it fails the run.\n\n**Refusing everything fails.** Declaring every trace unreplayable is safe and completes no work.\n\n===== SPEC.md =====\n# Replay specification (normative)\n\n## The rules\n\n| code | rule |\n|---|---|\n| `R1_SELECTOR_RESOLVED_LIVE` | Every step's selector must be resolved against the live tree at replay time. The recorded node id is a hint, not an address. |\n| `R2_PRECONDITION_OBSERVED` | Before a step runs, its precondition must be OBSERVED — read the attribute, do not assume the recorded value still holds. The audit records what was observed. |\n| `R3_POSTCONDITION_OBSERVED` | After a step runs, its postcondition is recorded from what the application shows, not from what the recording expected. |\n| `R4_CONFIRMATION_OBSERVED` | A step whose target declares `data-opens-confirmation` must not complete until a confirmation has been observed and accepted. An absent dialog is not a confirmation. |\n| `R5_IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY` | Replaying a trace that already completed must not repeat an irreversible step. Keeping that record is your job; the harness does not keep it for you. |\n| `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` | `app.askModel` may not be called. A replay that needs a model is not a replay. |\n| `R7_AUDIT_EXPLAINS` | Every step reports the node it resolved, the precondition it observed, and — when it stopped — a named halt reason. |\n\n## Outcomes\n\n`completed`, `halted`, `unreplayable`\n\n- `completed` — every step ran.\n- `halted` — the page is in a state this trace cannot proceed from **right now**. Try later.\n- `unreplayable` — the trace can never replay as recorded and must be re-recorded.\n\nThose are three different operational instructions and grading distinguishes them. Reporting\n`halted` for a trace whose target is gone tells an operator to wait for something that will never\nhappen.\n\n## The mutation space\n\nThe live tree may differ from the recorded one in exactly these ways, at any depth in the trace:\n\n| mutation | what it does |\n|---|---|\n| `none` | nothing changed |\n| `attribute_renamed` | the target's `data-testid` was renamed |\n| `node_reordered` | siblings were reordered |\n| `node_wrapped` | the target was wrapped in a new element and **re-created, so its id changed** |\n| `text_changed` | visible text changed |\n| `node_removed` | the target is gone |\n\nAlso varying: whether a confirmation dialog is `absent`, `present` or `suppressed`; whether an\nasync region has settled; and whether the trace is replayed once or twice.\n\nNothing outside this list happens. A hidden scenario samples this space more densely than the\nexamples do and adds no new kind of change.\n\n## The facade\n\n```\nquery(selector) -> { node, matches, pending }\nattr(nodeId, name) -> string | null\nclick(nodeId) / type(nodeId, text) / submit(nodeId)\nconfirmationPresent() -> boolean\nacceptConfirmation()   // throws when none is presented\naskModel(question)     // works, recorded, and a rule violation\n```\n\n`query` returns `matches` so ambiguity is visible: more than one match means the selector no\nlonger identifies one node, and picking the first is a guess.\n\n`pending: true` means the region exists and has not finished loading — *not yet*, as opposed to\n*not there*. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TbtrKi3iTQgiDsNDS9iTPK","type":"tool_result","content":"===== starter/subject.mjs =====\n// Starter. Implement `replay` and export `subject`.\n//\n//   replay(trace, app) -> { traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason }\n//\n// step audit: { index, resolvedNodeId, preconditionObserved, postconditionObserved,\n//               confirmationObserved, ran, haltReason }\n\nexport const subject = {\n  id: \"starter\",\n  label: \"Starter stub — replace me\",\n\n  replay(trace, app) {\n    const steps = [];\n\n    for (const step of trace.steps) {\n      // TODO: resolve the selector LIVE. step.recordedNodeId is a hint and may be stale.\n      const { node, matches, pending } = app.query(step.selector);\n\n      if (node === null) {\n        steps.push({\n          index: step.index,\n          resolvedNodeId: null,\n          preconditionObserved: null,\n          postconditionObserved: null,\n          confirmationObserved: null,\n          ran: false,\n          haltReason: pending ? \"REGION_PENDING\" : \"SELECTOR_UNRESOLVED\",\n        });\n        return { traceId: trace.id, outcome: \"halted\", steps, unreplayableReason: null };\n      }\n\n      // TODO: observe the precondition, handle confirmations, guard irreversible steps.\n      steps.push({\n        index: step.index,\n        resolvedNodeId: node.id,\n        preconditionObserved: null,\n        postconditionObserved: null,\n        confirmationObserved: null,\n        ran: false,\n        haltReason: \"NOT_IMPLEMENTED\",\n      });\n      return { traceId: trace.id, outcome: \"halted\", steps, unreplayableReason: null };\n    }\n\n    return { traceId: trace.id, outcome: \"completed\", steps, unreplayableReason: null };\n  },\n};\n\n===== types.ts =====\n// Domain model for the challenge. Read-only — the grader uses these exact shapes.\n\n// The PUBLIC domain model for UI action record-and-replay.\n//\n// Shipped verbatim in the agent-facing package, so nothing here is ground truth. The scenario, its\n// mutations and the effect ledger live in `truth.ts`.\n//\n// THE THESIS\n//\n// A model can discover a UI workflow. That is not the capability worth having. The capability worth\n// having is a RECORDING that replays deterministically with no model in the loop — because a\n// workflow that needs the model every time is not automation, it is a demo with a subscription.\n//\n// So the task is not \"click the right thing\". It is: record a trace whose every step carries what\n// must be true before it and what must be true after it, then replay that trace against a UI that\n// has changed underneath you, and either complete it or halt with a named reason. Improvising is a\n// failure even when it works — `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` is a rule, and the harness owns the channel\n// that would let you break it.\n\nexport interface UiNode {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly role: string;\n  readonly attrs: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\n  readonly text: string;\n  readonly children: readonly UiNode[];\n}\n\n/**\n * How a step finds its target.\n *\n * Structural rather than textual on purpose: matching on visible text would make the family a string\n * exercise, and text is the one thing that legitimately changes between record and replay.\n */\nexport interface Selector {\n  readonly kind: \"testid\" | \"role_index\" | \"attr\";\n  readonly value: string;\n  /** For `attr`: which attribute. For `role_index`: the index within the role. */\n  readonly qualifier: string | null;\n}\n\nexport const ACTION_KINDS = [\"click\", \"type\", \"submit\", \"confirm\"] as const;\nexport type ActionKind = (typeof ACTION_KINDS)[number];\n\n/** What must hold before a step runs. A step with no precondition is a step that cannot halt safely. */\nexport interface Precondition {\n  readonly nodeExists: boolean;\n  /** Attribute the target must carry, and the value it must have. */\n  readonly attr: string | null;\n  readonly attrValue: string | null;\n}\n\n/** What must hold after. Observed, never assumed. */\nexport interface Postcondition {\n  readonly effect: string | null;\n  readonly attr: string | null;\n  readonly attrValue: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface RecordedStep {\n  readonly index: number;\n  readonly kind: ActionKind;\n  readonly selector: Selector;\n  /**\n   * The node id this step resolved to WHEN IT WAS RECORDED.\n   *\n   * Present because real recordings store it, and because it is the temptation the family exists to\n   * measure: replaying against this id is faster, simpler, and wrong the moment the page changes.\n   * A correct replayer treats it as a hint and resolves the selector live.\n   */\n  readonly recordedNodeId: string;\n  readonly value: string | null;\n  readonly precondition: Precondition;\n  readonly postcondition: Postcondition;\n  /** True when running this step twice would do the thing twice. */\n  readonly irreversible: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface ActionTrace {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly steps: readonly RecordedStep[];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- what a replayer may do\n\nexport interface QueryResult {\n  readonly node: UiNode | null;\n  /** How many nodes matched. More than one is ambiguous and must not be resolved by guessing. */\n  readonly matches: number;\n  /**\n   * True when the region exists but has not finished loading.\n   *\n   * The distinction between \"not there yet\" and \"not there\" is the whole of `asyncSettled`, and it\n   * has to be readable: a replayer that cannot tell them apart must either halt on everything absent\n   * or re-record on everything absent, and both are wrong half the time. A real DOM exposes the same\n   * thing through `aria-busy`.\n   */\n  readonly pending: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * The application facade.\n *\n * `askModel` exists and works. Using it is a rule violation, and the harness records every call —\n * which is what makes \"no model in the loop\" a measurement rather than an honour system. A facade\n * that simply omitted the method would measure nothing: the interesting failure is an implementation\n * that reaches for a model when the deterministic path gets hard.\n */\nexport interface AppFacade {\n  /** Resolve a selector against the LIVE tree, right now. */\n  query: (selector: Selector) => QueryResult;\n  /** Read one attribute of a node, live. */\n  attr: (nodeId: string, name: string) => string | null;\n  click: (nodeId: string) => void;\n  type: (nodeId: string, text: string) => void;\n  submit: (nodeId: string) => void;\n  /** Observe whether a confirmation is currently presented. */\n  confirmationPresent: () => boolean;\n  /** Accept the presented confirmation. Throws if none is presented. */\n  acceptConfirmation: () => void;\n  /** Available, recorded, and a rule violation to call. */\n  askModel: (question: string) => string;\n}\n\nexport const OUTCOMES = [\"completed\", \"halted\", \"unreplayable\"] as const;\nexport type Outcome = (typeof OUTCOMES)[number];\n\nexport interface StepAudit {\n  readonly index: number;\n  /** The node id the selector actually resolved to at replay time, or null when it did not. */\n  readonly resolvedNodeId: string | null;\n  /** What the precondition check actually observed. Not \"ok\" — the observed value. */\n  readonly preconditionObserved: string | null;\n  readonly postconditionObserved: string | null;\n  readonly confirmationObserved: boolean | null;\n  readonly ran: boolean;\n  /** Why the step did not run, when it did not. Named reasons only. */\n  readonly haltReason: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface ReplayReport {\n  readonly traceId: string;\n  readonly outcome: Outcome;\n  readonly steps: readonly StepAudit[];\n  /** Set when the whole trace was refused as unreplayable, with the reason. */\n  readonly unreplayableReason: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Subject {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly label: string;\n  /**\n   * Replay one recorded trace against a live app.\n   *\n   * Called once per replay. A scenario may call it twice with the same trace, which is the\n   * idempotency test: the second run must not repeat an irreversible step.\n   */\n  readonly replay: (trace: ActionTrace, app: AppFacade) => ReplayReport;\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c7eac779-cc85-4dcb-8f32-841e52ef04dd","uuid":"b107f9d2-f5af-4b31-831c-5bd2644eeeea","timestamp":"2026-09-05T07:16:38.480Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"===== starter/subject.mjs =====\n// Starter. The capability worth\n// having is a RECORDING that replays deterministically with no model in the loop — because a\n// workflow that needs the model every time is not automation, it is a demo with a subscription.\n//\n// So the task is not \"click the right thing\". It is: record a trace whose every step carries what\n// must be true before it and what must be true after it, then replay that trace against a UI that\n// has changed underneath you, and either complete it or halt with a named reason. Improvising is a\n// failure even when it works — `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` is a rule, and the harness owns the channel\n// that would let you break it.\n\nexport interface UiNode {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly role: string;\n  readonly attrs: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\n  readonly text: string;\n  readonly children: readonly UiNode[];\n}\n\n/**\n * How a step finds its target.\n *\n * Structural rather than textual on purpose: matching on visible text would make the family a string\n * exercise, and text is the one thing that legitimately changes between record and replay.\n */\nexport interface Selector {\n  readonly kind: \"testid\" | \"role_index\" | \"attr\";\n  readonly value: string;\n  /** For `attr`: which attribute. For `role_index`: the index within the role. */\n  readonly qualifier: string | null;\n}\n\nexport const ACTION_KINDS = [\"click\", \"type\", \"submit\", \"confirm\"] as const;\nexport type ActionKind = (typeof ACTION_KINDS)[number];\n\n/** What must hold before a step runs. A step with no precondition is a step that cannot halt safely. */\nexport interface Precondition {\n  readonly nodeExists: boolean;\n  /** Attribute the target must carry, and the value it must have. */\n  readonly attr: string | null;\n  readonly attrValue: string | null;\n}\n\n/** What must hold after. Observed, never assumed. */\nexport interface Postcondition {\n  readonly effect: string | null;\n  readonly attr: string | null;\n  readonly attrValue: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface RecordedStep {\n  readonly index: number;\n  readonly kind: ActionKind;\n  readonly selector: Selector;\n  /**\n   * The node id this step resolved to WHEN IT WAS RECORDED.\n   *\n   * Present because real recordings store it, and because it is the temptation the family exists to\n   * measure: replaying against this id is faster, simpler, and wrong the moment the page changes.\n   * A correct replayer treats it as a hint and resolves the selector live.\n   */\n  readonly recordedNodeId: string;\n  readonly value: string | null;\n  readonly precondition: Precondition;\n  readonly postcondition: Postcondition;\n  /** True when running this step twice would do the thing twice. */\n  readonly irreversible: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface ActionTrace {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly steps: readonly RecordedStep[];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- what a replayer may do\n\nexport interface QueryResult {\n  readonly node: UiNode | null;\n  /** How many nodes matched. More than one is ambiguous and must not be resolved by guessing. */\n  readonly matches: number;\n  /**\n   * True when the region exists but has not finished loading.\n   *\n   * The distinction between \"not there yet\" and \"not there\" is the whole of `asyncSettled`, and it\n   * has to be readable: a replayer that cannot tell them apart must either halt on everything absent\n   * or re-record on everything absent, and both are wrong half the time. A real DOM exposes the same\n   * thing through `aria-busy`.\n   */\n  readonly pending: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * The application facade.\n *\n * `askModel` exists and works. Using it is a rule violation, and the harness records every call —\n * which is what makes \"no model in the loop\" a measurement rather than an honour system. A facade\n * that simply omitted the method would measure nothing: the interesting failure is an implementation\n * that reaches for a model when the deterministic path gets hard.\n */\nexport interface AppFacade {\n  /** Resolve a selector against the LIVE tree, right now. */\n  query: (selector: Selector) => QueryResult;\n  /** Read one attribute of a node, live. */\n  attr: (nodeId: string, name: string) => string | null;\n  click: (nodeId: string) => void;\n  type: (nodeId: string, text: string) => void;\n  submit: (nodeId: string) => void;\n  /** Observe whether a confirmation is currently presented. */\n  confirmationPresent: () => boolean;\n  /** Accept the presented confirmation. Throws if none is presented. */\n  acceptConfirmation: () => void;\n  /** Available, recorded, and a rule violation to call. */\n  askModel: (question: string) => string;\n}\n\nexport const OUTCOMES = [\"completed\", \"halted\", \"unreplayable\"] as const;\nexport type Outcome = (typeof OUTCOMES)[number];\n\nexport interface StepAudit {\n  readonly index: number;\n  /** The node id the selector actually resolved to at replay time, or null when it did not. */\n  readonly resolvedNodeId: string | null;\n  /** What the precondition check actually observed. Not \"ok\" — the observed value. */\n  readonly preconditionObserved: string | null;\n  readonly postconditionObserved: string | null;\n  readonly confirmationObserved: boolean | null;\n  readonly ran: boolean;\n  /** Why the step did not run, when it did not. Named reasons only. */\n  readonly haltReason: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface ReplayReport {\n  readonly traceId: string;\n  readonly outcome: Outcome;\n  readonly steps: readonly StepAudit[];\n  /** Set when the whole trace was refused as unreplayable, with the reason. */\n  readonly unreplayableReason: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Subject {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly label: string;\n  /**\n   * Replay one recorded trace against a live app.\n   *\n   * Called once per replay. A scenario may call it twice with the same trace, which is the\n   * idempotency test: the second run must not repeat an irreversible step.\n   */\n  readonly replay: (trace: ActionTrace, app: AppFacade) => ReplayReport;\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PcHkpLC3PTyjLd4vgvksrm","type":"tool_result","content":"subject.mjs\n// Deterministic replayer for recorded UI action traces.\n//\n// Design, against the rules in SPEC.md:\n//\n//   R1  every selector is resolved with app.query() at replay time; `recordedNodeId` is never\n//       used as an address, only ever compared against nothing at all.\n//   R2  every precondition is read back through app.attr() immediately before its step runs,\n//       and the OBSERVED value (not a verdict) is what the audit carries.\n//   R3  postconditions are read back from the live node after the step, same way.\n//   R4  a target that declares `data-opens-confirmation` only completes once a confirmation has\n//       actually been observed and accepted. No dialog => the step does not complete.\n//   R5  irreversible steps are recorded in a process-level ledger keyed by trace id, so a second\n//       replay re-observes everything but re-fires nothing.\n//   R6  app.askModel is never referenced.\n//   R7  every audited step carries the node it resolved, what it observed, and a named reason\n//       when it stopped.\n//\n// Outcome discipline:\n//   * target gone / selector no longer identifies one node  -> `unreplayable` (re-record)\n//   * region still loading, state not right yet, promised\n//     confirmation never appeared                            -> `halted` (try later)\n// A trace that cannot be replayed is detected by a read-only pre-flight pass over every step\n// BEFORE anything is clicked, so it is reported as unreplayable rather than half-run.\n\nconst LEDGER_KEY = Symbol.for(\"ui-action-record-replay.subject.ledger.v1\");\n\nconst HALT = {\n  PENDING: \"REGION_PENDING\",\n  UNRESOLVED: \"SELECTOR_UNRESOLVED\",\n  AMBIGUOUS: \"SELECTOR_AMBIGUOUS\",\n  PRECONDITION: \"PRECONDITION_MISMATCH\",\n  CONFIRMATION_ABSENT: \"CONFIRMATION_NOT_PRESENTED\",\n  CONFIRMATION_REFUSED: \"CONFIRMATION_NOT_ACCEPTED\",\n  ACTION_FAILED: \"ACTION_FAILED\",\n  UNSUPPORTED_KIND: \"UNSUPPORTED_ACTION_KIND\",\n  NOT_ATTEMPTED: \"NOT_ATTEMPTED\",\n};\n\nconst STATE_ATTR = \"data-state\";\nconst EFFECT_ATTR = \"data-effect\";\nconst CONFIRM_ATTR = \"data-opens-confirmation\";\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ irreversible-effect ledger\n//\n// The harness does not keep this for us (R5). It lives on globalThis so it survives a second\n// import of this module inside the same process.\n\nfunction ledgerStore() {\n  let store = globalThis[LEDGER_KEY];\n  if (!(store instanceof Map)) {\n    store = new Map();\n    try {\n      globalThis[LEDGER_KEY] = store;\n    } catch {\n      /* frozen global: fall back to a per-call map, still correct for a single import */\n    }\n  }\n  return store;\n}\n\nfunction ledgerFor(traceId) {\n  const store = ledgerStore();\n  let entry = store.get(traceId);\n  if (!entry) {\n    entry = { applied: new Map(), completed: false, runs: 0 };\n    store.set(traceId, entry);\n  }\n  return entry;\n}\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ facade, defensively wrapped\n//\n// A throwing facade must produce a named halt, never an exploded replay.\n\nfunction safeQuery(app, selector) {\n  try {\n    const result = app.query(selector);\n    if (!result || typeof result !== \"object\") return { node: null, matches: 0, pending: false };\n    const node = result.node ?? null;\n    const matches = typeof result.matches === \"number\" ? result.matches : node ? 1 : 0;\n    return { node, matches, pending: result.pending === true };\n  } catch {\n    return { node: null, matches: 0, pending: false };\n  }\n}\n\nfunction safeAttr(app, nodeId, name) {\n  if (nodeId == null || !name) return null;\n  try {\n    const value = app.attr(nodeId, name);\n    return value == null ? null : String(value);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction seesConfirmation(app) {\n  try {\n    return app.confirmationPresent() === true;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction takeConfirmation(app) {\n  try {\n    app.acceptConfirmation();\n    return true;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction performAction(app, step, nodeId) {\n  try {\n    switch (step.kind) {\n      case \"type\":\n        app.type(nodeId, step.value == null ? \"\" : String(step.value));\n        return \"ok\";\n      case \"click\":\n        app.click(nodeId);\n        return \"ok\";\n      case \"submit\":\n        app.submit(nodeId);\n        return \"ok\";\n      case \"confirm\":\n        return \"ok\"; // the confirmation itself is the action; handled by the caller\n      default:\n        return \"unsupported\";\n    }\n  } catch {\n    return \"failed\";\n  }\n}\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ observation helpers\n\nfunction resolvedId(query) {\n  // More than one match means the selector no longer identifies one node. Picking the first is a\n  // guess, so nothing is resolved.\n  if (query.matches > 1) return null;\n  return query.node && query.node.id != null ? query.node.id : null;\n}\n\n/** Read the precondition back off the live node. Returns the observed value, never a verdict. */\nfunction observePrecondition(app, step, nodeId) {\n  if (nodeId == null) return null;\n  const attr = step.precondition && step.precondition.attr ? step.precondition.attr : null;\n  return safeAttr(app, nodeId, attr || STATE_ATTR);\n}\n\nfunction preconditionSatisfied(step, observed) {\n  const pre = step.precondition || {};\n  if (!pre.attr) return true; // nothing declared beyond existence, which the caller checked\n  if (pre.attrValue == null) return observed !== null; // the attribute must merely be carried\n  return observed === pre.attrValue;\n}\n\n/** Read the postcondition back off the live node after the step (R3). */\nfunction observePostcondition(app, step, nodeId) {\n  if (nodeId == null) return null;\n  const post = step.postcondition || {};\n  if (post.attr) return safeAttr(app, nodeId, post.attr);\n  if (post.effect) return safeAttr(app, nodeId, EFFECT_ATTR) ?? safeAttr(app, nodeId, STATE_ATTR);\n  return safeAttr(app, nodeId, STATE_ATTR);\n}\n\nfunction declaresConfirmation(app, nodeId) {\n  const declared = safeAttr(app, nodeId, CONFIRM_ATTR);\n  return declared !== null && declared !== \"\" && declared !== \"false\";\n}\n\nfunction makeAudit(index, fields) {\n  return {\n    index,\n    resolvedNodeId: fields.resolvedNodeId ?? null,\n    preconditionObserved: fields.preconditionObserved ?? null,\n    postconditionObserved: fields.postconditionObserved ?? null,\n    confirmationObserved: fields.confirmationObserved ?? null,\n    ran: fields.ran === true,\n    haltReason: fields.haltReason ?? null,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction report(traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason = null) {\n  return { traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason };\n}\n\n/**\n * A `confirm` step targets a dialog that only exists once an earlier step opened it, and a step\n * that records `nodeExists: false` never expected its target to be there. Neither can be judged\n * by a pre-flight pass over the resting page.\n */\nfunction preflightApplies(step) {\n  if (step.kind === \"confirm\") return false;\n  if (step.precondition && step.precondition.nodeExists === false) return false;\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ pre-flight\n//\n// Read-only. Resolves every step's selector against the live tree before a single action fires, so\n// \"this trace can never replay as recorded\" is reported as such instead of discovered half way\n// through. `pending` is explicitly NOT fatal here: not-yet is not gone.\n\nfunction preflight(app, steps, ledger) {\n  const seen = [];\n  for (const step of steps) {\n    if (!preflightApplies(step) || ledger.applied.has(step.index)) {\n      seen.push({ index: step.index, resolvedNodeId: null, preconditionObserved: null });\n      continue;\n    }\n    const query = safeQuery(app, step.selector);\n    if (query.matches > 1) {\n      return { step, reason: HALT.AMBIGUOUS, seen };\n    }\n    if (query.node === null) {\n      if (query.pending) {\n        // The region exists and has not settled. That is a \"not yet\", decided at run time.\n        seen.push({ index: step.index, resolvedNodeId: null, preconditionObserved: null });\n        continue;\n      }\n      return { step, reason: HALT.UNRESOLVED, seen };\n    }\n    const nodeId = resolvedId(query);\n    seen.push({\n      index: step.index,\n      resolvedNodeId: nodeId,\n      preconditionObserved: observePrecondition(app, step, nodeId),\n    });\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction unreplayableReport(traceId, failure) {\n  const steps = [];\n  for (const observed of failure.seen) {\n    steps.push(\n      makeAudit(observed.index, {\n        resolvedNodeId: observed.resolvedNodeId,\n        preconditionObserved: observed.preconditionObserved,\n        ran: false,\n        haltReason: HALT.NOT_ATTEMPTED,\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n  steps.push(\n    makeAudit(failure.step.index, { resolvedNodeId: null, ran: false, haltReason: failure.reason }),\n  );\n  return report(traceId, Deterministic replayer for recorded UI action traces.\n//\n// Design, against the rules in SPEC.md:\n//\n//   R1  every selector is resolved with app.query() at replay time; `recordedNodeId` is never\n//       used as an address, only ever compared against nothing at all.\n//   R2  every precondition is read back through app.attr() immediately before its step runs,\n//       and the OBSERVED value (not a verdict) is what the audit carries.\n//   R3  postconditions are read back from the live node after the step, same way.\n//   R4  a target that declares `data-opens-confirmation` only completes once a confirmation has\n//       actually been observed and accepted. No dialog => the step does not complete.\n//   R5  irreversible steps are recorded in a process-level ledger keyed by trace id, so a second\n//       replay re-observes everything but re-fires nothing.\n//   R6  app.askModel is never referenced.\n//   R7  every audited step carries the node it resolved, what it observed, and a named reason\n//       when it stopped.\n//\n// Outcome discipline:\n//   * target gone / selector no longer identifies one node  -> `unreplayable` (re-record)\n//   * region still loading, state not right yet, promised\n//     confirmation never appeared                            -> `halted` (try later)\n// A trace that cannot be replayed is detected by a read-only pre-flight pass over every step\n// BEFORE anything is clicked, so it is reported as unreplayable rather than half-run.\n\nconst LEDGER_KEY = Symbol.for(\"ui-action-record-replay.subject.ledger.v1\");\n\nconst HALT = {\n  PENDING: \"REGION_PENDING\",\n  UNRESOLVED: \"SELECTOR_UNRESOLVED\",\n  AMBIGUOUS: \"SELECTOR_AMBIGUOUS\",\n  PRECONDITION: \"PRECONDITION_MISMATCH\",\n  CONFIRMATION_ABSENT: \"CONFIRMATION_NOT_PRESENTED\",\n  CONFIRMATION_REFUSED: \"CONFIRMATION_NOT_ACCEPTED\",\n  ACTION_FAILED: \"ACTION_FAILED\",\n  UNSUPPORTED_KIND: \"UNSUPPORTED_ACTION_KIND\",\n  NOT_ATTEMPTED: \"NOT_ATTEMPTED\",\n};\n\nconst STATE_ATTR = \"data-state\";\nconst EFFECT_ATTR = \"data-effect\";\nconst CONFIRM_ATTR = \"data-opens-confirmation\";\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ irreversible-effect ledger\n//\n// The harness does not keep this for us (R5). It lives on globalThis so it survives a second\n// import of this module inside the same process.\n\nfunction ledgerStore() {\n  let store = globalThis[LEDGER_KEY];\n  if (!(store instanceof Map)) {\n    store = new Map();\n    try {\n      globalThis[LEDGER_KEY] = store;\n    } catch {\n      /* frozen global: fall back to a per-call map, still correct for a single import */\n    }\n  }\n  return store;\n}\n\nfunction ledgerFor(traceId) {\n  const store = ledgerStore();\n  let entry = store.get(traceId);\n  if (!entry) {\n    entry = { applied: new Map(), completed: false, runs: 0 };\n    store.set(traceId, entry);\n  }\n  return entry;\n}\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ facade, defensively wrapped\n//\n// A throwing facade must produce a named halt, never an exploded replay.\n\nfunction safeQuery(app, selector) {\n  try {\n    const result = app.query(selector);\n    if (!result || typeof result !== \"object\") return { node: null, matches: 0, pending: false };\n    const node = result.node ?? null;\n    const matches = typeof result.matches === \"number\" ? result.matches : node ? 1 : 0;\n    return { node, matches, pending: result.pending === true };\n  } catch {\n    return { node: null, matches: 0, pending: false };\n  }\n}\n\nfunction safeAttr(app, nodeId, name) {\n  if (nodeId == null || !name) return null;\n  try {\n    const value = app.attr(nodeId, name);\n    return value == null ? null : String(value);\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction seesConfirmation(app) {\n  try {\n    return app.confirmationPresent() === true;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction takeConfirmation(app) {\n  try {\n    app.acceptConfirmation();\n    return true;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction performAction(app, step, nodeId) {\n  try {\n    switch (step.kind) {\n      case \"type\":\n        app.type(nodeId, step.value == null ? \"\" : String(step.value));\n        return \"ok\";\n      case \"click\":\n        app.click(nodeId);\n        return \"ok\";\n      case \"submit\":\n        app.submit(nodeId);\n        return \"ok\";\n      case \"confirm\":\n        return \"ok\"; // the confirmation itself is the action; handled by the caller\n      default:\n        return \"unsupported\";\n    }\n  } catch {\n    return \"failed\";\n  }\n}\n\n// ------------------------------------------------------------------ observation helpers\n\nfunction resolvedId(query) {\n  // More than one match means the selector no longer identifies one node. Picking the first is a\n  // guess, so nothing is resolved.\n  if (query.matches > 1) return null;\n  return query.node && query.node.id != null ? query.node.id : null;\n}\n\n/** Read the precondition back off the live node. Returns the observed value, never a verdict. */\nfunction observePrecondition(app, step, nodeId) {\n  if (nodeId == null) return null;\n  const attr = step.precondition && step.precondition.attr ? step.precondition.attr : null;\n  return safeAttr(app, nodeId, attr || STATE_ATTR);\n}\n\nfunction preconditionSatisfied(step, observed) {\n  const pre = step.precondition || {};\n  if (!pre.attr) return true; // nothing declared beyond existence, which the caller checked\n  if (pre.attrValue == null) return observed !== null; // the attribute must merely be carried\n  return observed === pre.attrValue;\n}\n\n/** Read the postcondition back off the live node after the step (R3). */\nfunction observePostcondition(app, step, nodeId) {\n  if (nodeId == null) return null;\n  const post = step.postcondition || {};\n  if (post.attr) return safeAttr(app, nodeId, post.attr);\n  if (post.effect) return safeAttr(app, nodeId, EFFECT_ATTR) ?? safeAttr(app, nodeId, STATE_ATTR);\n  return safeAttr(app, nodeId, STATE_ATTR);\n}\n\nfunction declaresConfirmation(app, nodeId) {\n  const declared = safeAttr(app, nodeId, CONFIRM_ATTR);\n  return declared !== null && declared !== \"\" && declared !== \"false\";\n}\n\nfunction makeAudit(index, fields) {\n  return {\n    index,\n    resolvedNodeId: fields.resolvedNodeId ?? null,\n    preconditionObserved: fields.preconditionObserved ?? null,\n    postconditionObserved: fields.postconditionObserved ?? null,\n    confirmationObserved: fields.confirmationObserved ?? null,\n    ran: fields.ran === true,\n    haltReason: fields.haltReason ?? null,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction report(traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason = null) {\n  return { traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason };\n}\n\n/**\n * A `confirm` step targets a dialog that only exists once an earlier step opened it, and a step\n * that records `nodeExists: false` never expected its target to be there. // A trace that already ran to completion is re-observed, never re-fired (R5).\n    if (ledger.completed) return replayCompleted(trace, app, ledger);\n\n    // Nothing has been touched yet: decide replayability before acting, so a dead trace is reported\n    // rather than half-run.\n    const failure = preflight(app, recorded, ledger);\n    if (failure) return unreplayableReport(traceId,
